Maybe simpler c_new = d + beta*(c-d) and clip to [0,tau], renormalize by scaling down peaks? Let's design stable:
```
def make_c_target(c, n, N, mix, tau=None):
    L=2*n-1; c=c.copy(); S=c[:L].sum(); if S>0: c[:L]=c[:L]/S
    d = np.zeros(N); d[:L]=1.0/L
    if mix>0: c = (1-mix)*c + mix*d
    if tau is not None and tau>0:
       # waterfill cap
       x = np.minimum(c, tau)  # c nonneg
       Sx=x.sum()
       if Sx < 1 - 1e-12:
          sim = 1 - Sx
          room = tau - x
          rsum = room.sum()
          if rsum > 0: x += sim*room/rsum
       elif Sx > 1 + 1e-12:
          sim = Sx - 1
          msum = x.sum()
          if msum >0: x -= sim*x/msum
       # for indices >=L, should be zero target? We can set x[L:]=0? But c below L only matters? Circular conv zeros after L. DFT of c with nonzero after L would break linearity? If we put mass after L, C corresponds to circular conv with wrap? But a support length n, linear conv after L must be zero. Let's design the c-surgery optimizer in detail.

`csurgery(a, n, N, C_d, d, max_iters, mix_start, mix_end, cap_start, cap_end, adapt=True)` maybe top-level. But many parameters. Could have simpler.

Pseudo:
```
def csurgery(a, n, N, C_d, timevec, max_iters=..., mix=0.2, cap_mode='adaptive'):
    a = np.maximum(a,0).astype(float)
    S=a.sum()
    if S<1e-12: a=np.ones(n)/n
    a/=S
    L=2*n-1
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_fast(a)
    eps=1e-12
    for it in range(max_iters):
        A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        C = A*A
        # time c current (first L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(C, N)[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c,0.0)
        S_c = c.sum()
        if S_c > 0: c = c/S_c
        # create c2
        c2 = mix_with_d(c, C_d? Actually want time d)
```
Precompute d_time length N: zeros N, first L =1/L.
`c2 = (1-mix)*c_padded + mix*d_time`.
Cap/waterfill.

Then update A:
```
# compute ratio for indices where |C| > threshold
C_safe = C.copy()
mask = np.abs(C) > 1e-10
ratio = np.ones_like(C)
ratio[mask] = C2[mask] / C[mask]
# sqrt ratio near 1
s = np.sqrt(ratio)
# flip where s is farther from 1 than -s
flip = np.abs(s - 1.0) > np.abs(s + 1.0)
s[flip] = -s[flip]
A_new = A * s
# for mask false, maybe set A_new = 0? Or keep A? If C small, use sqrt(C2) with no reference.
if (~mask).any():
    s0 = np.sqrt(C2[~mask])
    # choose sign? At DC, positive. For Nyq, positive. For others maybe principal.
    A_new[~mask] = s0[~mask]
```
But `s` computed for all with ratio for nonzero; we can use `np.sqrt(C)`? Wait for mask true ratio near 1. For mask false, fallback. However flipping per frequency based on s close to 1 is natural. Need ensure DC: C[0]=1, C2[0]=1, ratio=1, s=1. Good.
Then `a_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new,N)[:n]`. Since A_new may not be exactly Hermitian if sign flips independent in rfft? rfft sign flips are within nonnegative frequencies; the inverse assumes negative frequencies as conjugates of positive. If for a positive j we choose s, the corresponding negative is conj(s) * A[-j]? Since rfft stores only positive; irfft uses conjugate symmetry automatically: it reconstructs full A with A[-j]=conj(A[j]). If A_old was Hermitian and C2 Hermitian, ratio for j and N-j conjugates. But in rfft we only process j; sign flip at j may make A[j] not produce conjugate? The full spectrum will be set with A[-j]=conj(A[j]) by irfft, which may not equal applying same flip to original negative (which would be conj(s) if s chosen). But because C2[N-j]=conj(C2[j]), if we choose s (not necessarily satisfying conj consistency), the implied full C would have A[-j]^2 = conj(A[j])^2 = conj(A[j]^2) = conj(C2[j])? If A_new[j]=s*A[j], then A_new[j]^2 = s^2 C[j] = C2[j]. For negative, conj gives conj(C2[j]) = C2[N-j]. So it's consistent regardless of sign flip at positive frequency, because the negative is forced conjugate. Good.

Potential issue: `np.sqrt(ratio)` for complex ratio near 1 but ratio may be negative real with principal sqrt i, flip condition might choose -i? Both far from 1? If ratio has large phase (C2 not close), branch arbitrary; okay.

Let's consider using a target maximum τ and solve c2 by waterfill instead of d mix. We can implement a function `project_c(c, L, tau, mix_d=0.0)`:
```
x = c.copy()
if mix_d>0: x = (1-mix_d)*x + mix_d*(1/L)
if tau is not None:
   x = np.maximum(x,0)
   # upper cap with proportional addition
   upper = np.clip(x,0,tau)
   S=upper.sum()
   if S<1:
      room = tau - upper
      rsum=room.sum()
      if rsum<=0: upper = np.full(L,1.0/L)
      else: upper += (1-S)*room/rsum
   elif S>1:
      msum=upper.sum()
      upper -= (S-1)*upper/msum
   # ensure nonneg, cap again
   x = np.clip(upper,0,tau)
   # renormalize? If clip after addition maybe sum<1. Could loop twice.
```
Since room addition proportional to room may overshoot? If δ ≤ rsum, x_final = upper + δ*room/rsum. Some entries may exceed tau if room for them large? `upper_i + δ*room_i/rsum ≤ upper_i + room_i (δ/rsum) = upper_i + room_i ≤ tau` if δ≤rsum. Good. No overshoot. If S>1, subtract proportional to upper: upper_i*(1 - δ/sum) ≥0, and ≤tau. Sum exactly. Great. So one step exact sum and bounds.
If mix_d used before, sum of x=1. After clip S≤1, addition. Good.

This waterfill is not closest L2 but structured. If τ near average, room large for small entries; adding proportional to room fills low entries more (since bigger room), raising valleys. Good.

Top-level helpers:
```
def softmax_from_theta_parts(z): # z length n-1
    n = len(z)+1
    th = np.empty(n)
    th[0]=0.0
    th[1:]=z
    m = np.max(th)
    e = np.exp(th - m)
    a = e / e.sum()
    return a
```

- `def _next_pow2(m): return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()` but if m<=1 return 1. Need top-level no lambda.

```
def convolution_from_a(a, N):
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    C = A*A
    L = 2*len(a)-1
    c = np.fft.irfft(C, N)[:L]
    return c, C
```
- `def score_sequence(a):` returns score. Could call evaluate? Internal.
```
def internal_score(a):
   n=len(a); S=a.sum(); if S<1e-12 return inf
   c, _ = convolution_from_a(a, fft_len(n))
   return 2*n*max(c)/S/S
```
Since we normalize sum=1, score=2n maxc.

- `def normalize(a):` clip nonneg, sum to 1.
```
def make_nonneg_normalized(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: a=np.ones_like(a)*0.5
    a=a/s
    return a
```
But for large n, all ones /n.

- `def build_inits(n, rng, parent, N):` returns list of arrays.

```
def beta_like(n, alpha):
    z = (np.arange(n)+0.5)/n

```
def run_csurgery(a, N, d, max_iters, mix_start, mix_end, target_score, deadline, tag):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    a = make_nonneg_normalized(a)
    dL = d[:L] # d length N; assume first L 1/L
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_a(a,N)
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if it%50==0 and tag: print...
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       A = np.fft.rfft(a,N)
       C = A*A
       c = np.fft.irfft(C,N)[:L]
       c = np.maximum(c,0)
       scurr = 2*n*float(c.max())
       if scurr < best_sc - 1e-12: best... no_improve=0 else no_improve++
       progress=it/max(1,max_iters)
       mix = mix_start + (mix_end-mix_start)*progress
       # maybe if target_score, adapt mix: target_cmax=target_score/(2n); current cmax; if cmax > target, desired mix = (cmax - target)/(cmax - avg) if cmax>avg

       target_cmax = target_score/(2.0*n) if target_score else avg
       if target_cmax < avg: target_cmax=avg
       if c.max() > avg:
          mix_to_target = (cmax_left? )

Implementation details for ratio update:
```
C = A*A
mix = ...
C2 = (1.0 - mix) * C + mix * C_target
absC = np.abs(C)
mask = absC > 1e-12 / n? # threshold scale
# Since C[0]=1, values ~1/L? Actually A_j^2 magnitude typical? A(0)=1; nonzero A maybe 1/√n, square 1/n. So threshold 1e-12*n? Use 1e-14.
ratio = np.ones(C.shape, dtype=np.complex128)
ratio[mask] = C2[mask] / C[mask]
s = np.sqrt(ratio)
flip = np.abs(s - 1.0) > np.abs(s + 1.0)
s[flip] = -s[flip]
A_new = A * s
if not mask.all():
    s0 = np.sqrt(C2[~mask])
    # If any bad finite? replace nan with 0
    s0 = np.where(np.isfinite(s0), s0, 0.0j)
    A_new[~mask] = s0[~mask]
# Ensure DC positive real: A_new[0]=abs(A_new[0]) (should be 1)
A_new[0] = abs(A_new[0])
# Nyquist: if N even, A_new[-1] = abs(A_new[-1])? Since real sequence at Nyquist should be real. C2[-1] should positive? To be safe set real.
if (N % 2)==0:
    A_new[-1] = np.abs(A_new[-1])

```
def run_peak_descent(a, N, N2, max_iters, n_peaks, step_init, deadline, tag):
    a=make_nonneg_normalized; n=len; L=2n-1; lb=2n/(2n-1)
    best, best_sc
    step=step_init
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       A=rfft(a,N); C=A*A; c=irfft(C,N)[:L]
       cmax=c.max(); score=2n*cmax
       update
       if score <= lb+1e-12: break
       # top peaks
       if n_peaks>=L: top_idx=np.arange(L)
       else: top_idx=np.argpartition(c, -n_peaks)[-n_peaks:]
       p=np.zeros(L); p[top_idx]=1.0/n_peaks
       # remove tiny negative c? c can have tiny negative; top ok.
       p_rev=p[::-1]
       A2=rfft(a,N2); P=rfft(p_rev,N2); r=irfft(A2*P,N2); v=r[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
       grad=4.0*n*v
       # gradient may have negative/positive. We want descent: a_new = a - step*grad.
       # But grad maybe zero where? okay.
       # line search trial steps
       accepted=False
       for factor in [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625]:
           s=step*factor
           a2=a - s*grad
           a2=np.clip(a2,0.0,1000.0)
           S=a2.sum()
           if S<1e-12: continue
           a2/=S
           c2, _? compute score quickly
           sc2=score_from_a(a2,N)
           if sc2 < score - 1e-15:
               a=a2; step = min(step*1.01, 0.05); accepted=True; break
           elif sc2 < score: # maybe accept small? no
               pass
       if not accepted:
           # try tiny random? no; reduce step
           step *= 0.8
       if score improved: no_improve=0 else +=1
       if no_improve>200: # perturb
          a = make_nonneg_normalized(a * (1.0 + 0.01*rng? But no rng parameter? Use deterministic sine?))
```

`def compute_score(a, N=None):` if N None compute from n. Use `np.fft.rfft(a,N)`, C, c. But if N passed Nopt, first L same. Return score. We can also return cmax.
```
def score_from_a(a, N=None):
  n=len(a)
  if N is None: N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
  A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
  c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
  s=a.sum()
  if s<1e-12: return np.inf
  return 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))/(s*s)
```
Since normalized sum=1.

`def softmax_lse_fun(x, beta, N, Ngrad):` top-level. Need if x len 0? n=1? Not relevant. But handle.
```
def softmax_lse_fun(x, beta, N, Ngrad):
    n = len(x)+1
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, np.zeros(len(x))
    th0 = 0.0
    # build theta length n, but to avoid allocating extra? We can compute a by exp of x with x0=0 shifted.
    theta = np.empty(n); theta[0]=0; theta[1:]=x
    m = theta.max()
    e = np.exp(theta - m)
    se = e.sum()
    if se <= 0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
    a = e/se
    # Maybe if a has nonfinite return
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    C = A*A
    L=2*n-1
    c = np.fft.irfft(C, N)[:L]
    maxc = float(c.max())
    if maxc <= 0 or not np.isfinite(maxc): return 1e100, zeros
    shift = beta*(c - maxc)
    shift -= shift.max()
    expv = np.exp(shift)
    sumv = float(expv.sum())
    if sumv <= 0 or not np.isfinite(sumv):
        expv = np.zeros(L); expv[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; sumv=1.0
    Lsm = maxc + np.log(sumv)/beta
    p = expv/sumv
    # If beta huge, p has many zeros; gradient okay.
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    A2 = np.fft.rfft(a, Ngrad)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, Ngrad)
    r = np.fft.irfft(A2*P, Ngrad)
    v = r[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    grad_a = 4.0*n*v
    dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    u = a*(grad_a - dot)
    # ensure finite
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
       return 2.0*n*Lsm, np.zeros(len(x))
    grad = u[1:]
    # scale? If grad norm huge? L-BFGS uses.
    val = 2.0*n*Lsm
    return val, grad
```
Need note: For softmax with θ0=0, gradient wrt x is u[1:] only? Earlier derivation with θ0 fixed yes, u_full (gradient wrt θ full) and objective invariant to adding constant; if θ0 fixed, derivative wrt x_j = u_j (not u_j - u_0) because θ0 fixed, θ_j = x_j. But since softmax mapping [0,x] has flat direction removed by fixing θ0? Let's verify. a = softmax([0,x]) (with θ0=0). Let g_j = ∂F/∂θ_j for full θ (sum g=0). For mapping θ_0=0, θ_j=x_j for j>0. Derivative ∂F/∂x_j = g_j (j>0), since θ_0 fixed not variable. The full g computed as a*(grad_a - dot) has sum 0. Is it the gradient with respect to θ full in coordinates where additions? Yes. If we fix θ0=0, derivative is g_j, not g_j - g_0. Good. Our previous centering was for θ' = θ - θ0*1. If θ0 fixed to 0, x_j=θ_j; derivative g_j. Since full g sum zero, g_0 = -∑_{j>0}g_j. Fine. So return u[1:]. Good.

But if we internally subtract max m when computing softmax, does derivative still g_j? Softmax([0,x]) invariant; the chain rule with g computed from a works. Yes.

def build_inits(n, N_init, C_target, parent):
   inits=[]
   if parent: inits.append(resize_sequence(parent,n))
   # target pos/abs
   At=np.sqrt(C_target)
   a1=np.fft.irfft(At,N_init)[:n]
   pos=make_nonneg_normalized(np.maximum(a1,0))
   absa=make_nonneg_normalized(np.abs(a1))
   inits.append(pos); inits.append(absa)
   # beta alphas
   for alpha in [0.35,0.5,0.7]: inits.append(beta_like(n,alpha))
   # uniform
   inits.append(np.ones(n)/n)
   # maybe random spectrum positive? use rng? maybe skip to save
   return inits

```
def improve_pipeline(a, budget_s, deadline, target_score):
    a=make_nonneg_normalized(a); n=len(a); Nscore=next_pow2(2n-1)

def run_csurgery_suite(a, N, C_target, allocate, deadline, tag):
    a=make_nonneg_normalized(a)
    best=a; best_sc=score_from_a(a,N)
    # allocate segments fractions of allocate, but not exceed deadline
    segs = [(0.05,0.70,0.30),(0.20,0.85,0.30),(0.40,0.95,0.25),(0.70,1.0,0.15)]
    for ms,me,frac in segs:
       alloc = frac*allocate
       if alloc < 5: continue
       sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+alloc)
       # max_iters estimate: n expensive? Use 200000 but time.

       if sc2 < best_sc: best=a2; best_sc=sc2
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
    return best,best_sc
```

Let's modify target generator:
```
def make_target_rfft(n, N, mode='flat', p=5):
    L=2*n-1
    t=np.ones(L)
    if mode == 'bell':
        z = np.arange(L)/(L-1)
        y = np.abs(2*z - 1.0)
        # t = 1 - y**p, ensure nonneg
        t = 1.0 - y**p
        # if p not matched, can rescale to desired max ratio? Normalize anyway; max ratio after normalization = 1 / mean(t). We can choose p to approximate desired ratio. For target score ratio r=score/ (2n avg?)? Score target 1.5 -> max/avg=1.5 -> mean(t)=2/3. Solve p+1=2/(1-mean?)? mean(t)=1 - 2/(p+1). Set=2/3 -> p=5. Good.
    elif mode == 'cos':
        z=np.arange(L)/(L-1)
        t = 1.0 + 0.5*np.cos(np.pi*z) # edges 0.5, max1.5 ratio? Mean =0.75? Wait ∫ 1+0.5 cos(π z) z0..2 =2 +0.5*0? Integral cos over full? ∫_0^2 cos(π z) dz =0. So mean=1. Then max after normalization =1, ratio1? No, because mean=1 (since length2 average? In discrete sum normalized, avg=1/L. t mean=1. max=1.5? But sum average 1, max 1.5, ratio1.5 yes, because t values in [0.5,1.5], average 1. Actually discrete average exactly ~1 if cos integral zero; max/avg=1.5. Edges 0.5. Good.
    t=np.maximum(t,0)
    t=t/t.sum()
    d=np.zeros(N); d[:L]=t
    return np.fft.rfft(d,N)
```

1. `next_pow2(m)`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

2. `make_nonneg_normalized(a)`:
```
def make_nonneg_normalized(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size ==0: a=np.ones(64)
    a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        a=np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        if a.size==0: a=np.ones(64)
        s=float(a)
    a=a/s
    return a
```
If a has NaN, clip? np.clip NaN remains NaN. Need replace nonfinite:
```
a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
```
Do that.

3. `score_from_a(a, N=None)`:
```
def score_from_a(a,N=None):
    a=np.asarray(a)
    n=len(a); S=float(a.sum())
    if S<1e-12: return np.inf
    if N is None: N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:2*n-1]
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return 2.0*n*m/(S*S)
```
For normalized sum=1, okay. c may have tiny negative, max positive.

5. `resize_sequence(parent,n)`:
```
def resize_sequence(parent,n):
    p=np.asarray(parent,dtype=float).ravel()
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0); p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
    if len(p)==n: return make_nonneg_normalized(p)
    if len(p)==1: return np.full(n,p[0]/n?) normalize
    old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,len(p)); new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    q=np.interp(new, old, p)
    return make_nonneg_normalized(q)
```
If parent large, linear.

6. `beta_like(n, alpha)`:
```
def beta_like(n, alpha):
    z=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    base=z*(1.0-z)
    # avoid zero
    base=np.maximum(base, 1e-300)
    if alpha ==0: a=np.ones(n)
    else: a=np.power(base, -alpha)
    return make_nonneg_normalized(a)
```
For alpha negative? not.

7. `make_target_rfft(n,N,mode='rootbell')`:
```
def make_target_rfft(n,N,mode='rootbell'):
    L=2*n-1
    t=np.ones(L)
    if mode != 'flat':
       z=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,L)  # for L=1? n>=...
       y=np.abs(2.0*z - 1.0)
       if mode == 'rootbell':
           t = 1.0 - np.sqrt(np.clip(y,0.0,1.0))
       elif mode == 'cos05':
           t = 1.0 + 0.5*np.cos(np.pi*z)
       elif mode == 'cos1':
           t = 1.0 + np.cos(np.pi*z)
       elif mode == 'cos075':
           t = 1.0 + 0.75*np.cos(np.pi*z)
       elif mode.startswith('beta'):
           # parse alpha
           try: alpha=float(mode[4:])
           except: alpha=0.5
           a=beta_like(n, alpha)
           c,score =? Need convolution c. We can compute using a and N? Use minimal Nscore? For target length L, use N for conv.
           A=np.fft.rfft(a,N); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,N)[:L]
           c=np.maximum(c,0.0); s=c.sum(); if s>0: t=c/s
       elif mode == 'quadratic': # 1 - 0.5*y? etc
           t = 1.0 - 0.5*y
    t=np.maximum(t,0.0)
    s=float(t.sum())
    if s <=0 or not finite: t=np.ones(L)
    else: t=t/s
    d=np.zeros(N)
    d[:L]=t
    return np.fft.rfft(d,N).astype(np.complex128)
```
For z length L, `np.linspace(0,1,L)`. At endpoints y=±1 absolute. For cos modes, t may be slightly negative due roundoff; max.
For beta mode, computing convolution uses N maybe Nopt. If N not enough? N>=L yes. Good. Note `a` normalized sum=1, sum c=1, t=c/s. This target is exactly c distribution of beta a.

Could target beta have max ratio maybe >? Good.

def target_spectrum_inits(n,N,C_target):
    At=np.sqrt(C_target)
    # ensure DC positive
    At[0]=abs(At[0])
    a_full=np.fft.irfft(At,N)[:n]

    a2=make_nonneg_normalized(np.abs(a_full))

```

```
def run_csurgery(a, N, C_target, max_iters, mix_start, mix_end, deadline, tag='') :
    a=make_nonneg_normalized(a)
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1
    best=a.copy(); best_sc=score_from_a(a,N) # uses N
    no_improve=0
    eps=1e-14

           # if it > 500 and no improvement for 1000, break

11. `run_csurgery_suite` maybe not needed separately; process_length can call `run_csurgery` multiple segments. Let's implement `run_csurgery_passes(a, N, C_target, allocate, start_time, deadline, tag)`:

def run_csurgery_passes(a, N, C_target, allocate, deadline, tag):
    a=make_nonneg_normalized(a); best=a; best_sc=score_from_a(a,N)
    passes=[(0.05,0.70,0.30),(0.20,0.85,0.30),(0.40,0.95,0.25),(0.60,1.00,0.15)]
    t=start= time.monotonic()
    for ms,me,frac in passes:
       if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5: break
       sec = frac*allocate
       if sec < 2: continue
       sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic()+sec)
       a2,sc2 = run_csurgery(best,N,C_target,100000,ms,me,sub_deadline,time.monotonic(),sec,tag+' pass')
       if sc2 < best_sc: best=a2; best_sc=sc2
    return best,best_sc
```

12. `run_peak_descent`: Use Nscore for c, Ngrad for p. Signature with start_time/duration? It can just deadline.
```
def run_peak_descent(a, N, Ngrad, max_iters, n_peaks, step_init, deadline, tag=''):
    a=make_nonneg_normalized(a); n=len; L=2n-1; lb=2n/(2n-1)
    best=a; best_sc=score_from_a(a,N)
    step=step_init
    no_improve=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
        if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
        A=rfft(a,N); C=A*A; c=irfft(C,N)[:L]
        cmax=float(c.max()); score=2*n*cmax
        if score<best_sc: update no_improve=0 else no_improve++
        if no_improve>1000: break
        if n_peaks>=L: top=np.arange(L)
        else: top=np.argpartition(c, -n_peaks)[-n_peaks:]
        p=np.zeros(L); p[top]=1.0/len(top)
        # Optional: weight p by excess? p = (c-topmin)_+? For top K, use p proportional to c - min_top? Could focus max.
        # We can set p = np.exp((c-dc)/scale)? But top K okay.
        p_rev=p[::-1]
        A2=rfft(a,Ngrad); P=rfft(p_rev,Ngrad)
        r=irfft(A2*P,Ngrad)
        v=r[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
        grad=4*n*v
        gnorm=float(np.dot(grad,grad))
        if gnorm <1e-30: break
        max_step = (score - lb)/(gnorm + 1e-12)
        s=min(step, max(1e-6,max_step), 0.05)
        a2=a - s*grad
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a2)): s*=0.5
        a2=np.clip(a2,0,1000)
        S=a2.sum()
        if S<1e-12: break
        a2/=S
        sc2=score_from_a(a2,N)
        if sc2 < score - 1e-15:
            a=a2; step=min(step*1.01, 0.05)
        else:
            # try half
            s2=s*0.5
            a3=np.clip(a - s2*grad,0,1000)
            S3=a3.sum()
            if S3>0:
              a3/=S3; sc3=score_from_a(a3,N)
              if sc3<score: a=a3; step=s2? else step*=0.8
            else: step*=0.8
        # if no improvement in several, reduce n_peaks? no
    return best,best_sc
```
This uses extra score for half. Limit maybe. We can simplify: one trial; if fail step*=0.7. To save, use one. Keep one plus if no accept 50 times break.
```
if sc2 < score: accept else step*=0.75
if step<1e-6 break
```

Choose n_peaks for peak suite: [3,10,30,100]. Step [0.02,0.015,0.01,0.005].

`run_lbfgs`:
```
def run_lbfgs(a, beta, N, Ngrad, time_limit, deadline, tag=''):
    a=make_nonneg_normalized(a); n=len(a)
    if n < 2: return a, score
    # if time_limit small return
    eps = max(np.finfo(np.float64).tiny, 1e-12*float(a.max()))
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, eps))
    x0 = np.clip(theta[1:] - theta[0], -50, 50)
    val0,grad0 = softmax_lse_fun(x0,beta,N,Ngrad)
    if not finite val0: return a,score
    t_call = max(1e-3, time.monotonic()-t_start)
    allocated = min(time_limit, max(0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
    if allocated < 10: return a,score
    # estimate maxfun. L-BFGS may call fun maxiter+maxls? maxfun option caps.
    maxfun_est = int(allocated / (t_call*1.5))
    maxfun = min(2000, max(50, maxfun_est))
    maxiter = min(1000, max(20, int(allocated/(t_call*1.2))))
    res = minimize(softmax_lse_fun, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, args=(beta,N,Ngrad), options={'maxiter':maxiter,'maxfun':maxfun,'ftol':1e-12,'gtol':1e-9,'maxls':20,'disp':False})
    if res.success or res.status in [0,1,2,3]: ...
    x_res = res.x if finite else x0
    a2=theta_to_softmax(x_res)
    a2=make_nonneg_normalized(a2)

def process_length(n, parent, length_deadline, tag):
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    # if deadline too close return
    if time.monotonic() > length_deadline - 30: return None,inf? But top-level must return arrays.
    Nscore,Nopt,Ngrad = fft_lengths(n)
    # default target for initializations: rootbell
    C_main=make_target_rfft(n,Nopt,'rootbell')
    inits=build_inits(n,Nopt,C_main,parent)
    best=None; best_sc=np.inf
    # score inits
    for idx,a0 in enumerate(inits):
       a0=make_nonneg_normalized(a0)
       sc=score_from_a(a0,Nscore)
       print...
       if sc<best_sc: best=a0; best_sc=sc
    if best is None: best=np.ones(n)/n; best_sc=score...
    phase_total = max(60.0, length_deadline - t_start)
    # Reserve
    alloc_quick = 0.10 * phase_total
    alloc_targets = 0.50 * phase_total
    alloc_peak = 0.15 * phase_total
    alloc_lse = 0.10 * phase_total if n <= 10000 else 0.0
    # buffer 0.15
    if n > 12000: alloc_peak=0.10; alloc_lse=0
    # Quick passes on inits
    q_deadline = min(length_deadline, t_start + alloc_quick)
    per = max(1.0, alloc_quick / max(1,len(inits)))
    for a0 in inits:
       if time.monotonic() > q_deadline: break
       sub_deadline = min(q_deadline, time.monotonic()+per)
       a2,sc2 = run_csurgery(best? or a0? We want explore each init, but starting from best for later? Quick should test a0. Use a0.
       a2,sc2 = run_csurgery(a0,Nopt,C_main,100000,0.1,0.85,sub_deadline,time.monotonic(),max(0.1,sub_deadline-now),tag)
       if sc2<best_sc: best=a2; best_sc=sc2
    # Targeted csurgery passes
    target_deadline = min(length_deadline - (alloc_peak+alloc_lse+30.0), length_deadline)
    modes = [('rootbell',1.0), ('cos05',0.8), ('beta05',0.7), ('flat',0.5), ('cos1',0.4)]
    total_weight=sum(weights)
    for mode,w in modes:
       rem=target_deadline-time.monotonic(); if rem<10 break
       alloc=alloc_targets*(w/total_weight)
       sub_deadline=min(target_deadline, time.monotonic()+alloc)
       C=make_target_rfft(n,Nopt,mode)
       # maybe if best already below target, use smaller? no
       a2,sc2=run_csurgery_passes(best,Nopt,C,alloc,sub_deadline,tag+':'+mode)
       if sc2<best_sc: best=a2; best_sc=sc2
    # Peak
    if time.monotonic() < length_deadline - 30 and alloc_peak>0:
       p_deadline = min(length_deadline, time.monotonic()+alloc_peak)
       best,best_sc=run_peak_suite(best,Nscore,Ngrad,alloc_peak,p_deadline,tag)
    # LSE
    if time.monotonic() < length_deadline - 20 and alloc_lse>0:
       lse_deadline = min(length_deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic()+alloc_lse)
       best,best_sc=run_lse_suite(best,Nscore,Ngrad,alloc_lse,lse_deadline,tag)
    final_sc=score_from_a(best,Nscore)
    return best, final_sc
```

Let's implement `clean_rfft`:
```
def clean_rfft(A, N):
    A = np.array(A, dtype=np.complex128, copy=True)
    A[0] = abs(A[0])
    if (N % 2) == 0 and A.size > 0:
        A[-1] = float(np.real(A[-1]))
    bad = ~np.isfinite(A)
    if bad.any():
        A[bad] = 0.0j
    return A
```
`np.isfinite(A)` works for complex. Good.

In `make_target_rfft`, after C = rfft, call C=clean_rfft(C,N) but ensure Nyq abs before clean? clean sets Nyq real but keeps sign. We want abs. So:
```

if N%2==0: C[-1]=abs(C[-1])
C=clean_rfft(C,N)
```
Good.

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    final_deadline = t_start + total - 25.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed) # maybe unused except? no random in helpers. Could omit.
    parent = None
    try:
       p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()

          p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
          p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)

             parent = p
    except Exception:
       parent = None
    best_a = None; best_sc = np.inf
    if parent is not None:
       pa = make_nonneg_normalized(parent)
       Np = next_pow2(2*len(pa)-1)

    if 8192 not in quick_ns: quick_ns.append(8192)
    for n in quick_ns[:2]:

       alphas=[0.3,0.4,0.5,0.6]

Now, within process_length, after target, peak_lse. If target_deadline left time, we can use leftover for target? It uses loop until target_deadline; if last mode finishes early, unused goes to peak/lse because their deadlines computed from length_deadline and alloc. Good.

Potential inconsistency: `alloc_peak` reserved but if target finishes early, peak gets only its allocated, not leftover. Could pass `peak_deadline = min(length_deadline, time.monotonic() + max(alloc_peak, length_deadline - time.monotonic()))` to use leftover? We want not overrun if LSE. Could dynamically:
```

peak_alloc = min(alloc_peak + 0.5*left, left) # no
```

```
left = length_deadline - time.monotonic()
lse_reserve = 0.5*alloc_lse + 10 if alloc_lse>0 else 10
peak_alloc = min(alloc_peak + 0.5*left, max(0,left - lse_reserve))
peak_deadline = min(length_deadline, time.monotonic()+peak_alloc)

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2 n max(a*a) / (sum a)^2
using:
  1) analytic initializers (Beta-like endpoint singularities),
  2) GRASP-style projections toward convolution targets in the spectrum
     (target coefficients of a*a chosen to be near-flat or minimax-like),
  3) active-peak FFT subgradient descent,
  4) log-sum-exp smoothed L-BFGS polish in softmax parameters.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def make_nonneg_normalized(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def fft_lengths(n, cap_opt=32768):
    n = int(n)
    nsc = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    nopt = next_pow2(2 * (2 * n - 1))
    if nopt > cap_opt:
        nopt = max(nsc, cap_opt)
    ngrad = next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    return nsc, nopt, ngrad


def clean_rfft(A, N):
    A = np.array(A, dtype=np.complex128, copy=True)
    if A.size == 0:
        return A
    A[0] = abs(A[0])
    if (N % 2) == 0:
        A[-1] = float(np.real(A[-1]))
    bad = ~np.isfinite(A)
    if bad.any():
        A[bad] = 0.0j
    return A


def score_from_a(a, N=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    if N is None:
        N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:2 * n - 1]
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * n * m / (S * S)


def resize_sequence(parent, n):
    p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = int(n)
    if p.size == n:
        return make_nonneg_normalized(p)
    if p.size == 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    q = np.interp(new, old, p)
    return make_nonneg_normalized(q)


def beta_like(n, alpha):
    n = int(n)
    z = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    base = np.maximum(z * (1.0 - z), 1e-300)
    if abs(alpha) < 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = np.power(base, -float(alpha))
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    return make_nonneg_normalized(a)


def make_target_rfft(n, N, mode="rootbell"):
    n = int(n)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    if mode == "flat":
        t = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        z = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, L)
        y = np.abs(2.0 * z - 1.0)
        if mode == "rootbell":
            # Average 2/3, peak/average 3/2, zero endpoints.
            t = 1.0 - np.sqrt(np.clip(y, 0.0, 1.0))
        elif mode == "cos05":
            t = 1.0 + 0.5 * np.cos(np.pi * z)
        elif mode == "cos075":
            t = 1.0 + 0.75 * np.cos(np.pi * z)
        elif mode == "cos1":
            t = 1.0 + np.cos(np.pi * z)
        elif mode.startswith("beta"):
            try:
                alpha = float(mode[4:])
            except Exception:
                alpha = 0.5
            a = beta_like(n, alpha)
            A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
            c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, N)[:L]
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
            s = float(c.sum())
            if s > 1e-12:
                t = c / s
            else:
                t = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            t = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)

    t = np.where(np.isfinite(t), t, 0.0)
    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    s = float(t.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        t = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(L)
    t = t / s

    d = np.zeros(N, dtype=np.float64)
    d[:L] = t
    C = np.fft.rfft(d, N).astype(np.complex128)
    if (N % 2) == 0:
        C[-1] = abs(C[-1])
    return clean_rfft(C, N)


def target_spectrum_inits(n, N, C_target):
    At = np.sqrt(C_target)
    At = clean_rfft(At, N)
    a_full = np.fft.irfft(At, N)[:n]
    out = []
    a1 = np.maximum(a_full, 0.0)
    if a1.sum() > 1e-12:
        out.append(make_nonneg_normalized(a1))
    a2 = np.abs(a_full)
    if a2.sum() > 1e-12:
        out.append(make_nonneg_normalized(a2))
    return out


def build_inits(n, N, C_main, parent):
    inits = []
    if parent is not None:
        inits.append(resize_sequence(parent, n))
    for a in target_spectrum_inits(n, N, C_main):
        inits.append(a)
    for alpha in (0.35, 0.5, 0.7):
        inits.append(beta_like(n, alpha))
    inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n)
    return inits


def quick_beta_candidates(n, N, alphas):
    best = None
    best_sc = np.inf
    for alpha in alphas:
        a = beta_like(n, alpha)
        sc = score_from_a(a, N)
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = a
    return best, best_sc


def run_csurgery(a, N, C_target, max_iters, mix_start, mix_end,
                 deadline, start_time=0.0, duration=1.0, tag=""):
    a = make_nonneg_normalized(a)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_from_a(a, N)
    no_improve = 0

    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline:
            break

        A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        C = A * A
        c = np.fft.irfft(C, N)[:L]
        sc = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c))

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            no_improve = 0
            if tag:
                print(f"    {tag}: improve {sc:.8f} it={it} t={now-start_time:.1f}s")
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if duration > 0.0:
            progress = min(1.0, max(0.0, (now - start_time) / duration))
        else:
            progress = 1.0

        if no_improve > 1600 and progress > 0.85:
            break

        if tag and it > 0 and it % 700 == 0:
            print(f"    {tag}: current={sc:.8f} mix={progress:.2f} t={now-start_time:.1f}s")

        mix = mix_start + (mix_end - mix_start) * progress
        mix = min(1.0, max(0.0, mix))
        C2 = (1.0 - mix) * C + mix * C_target

        absC = np.abs(C)
        mask = absC > 1e-13
        ratio = np.ones(C.shape, dtype=np.complex128)
        if mask.any():
            ratio[mask] = C2[mask] / C[mask]

        s = np.sqrt(ratio)
        flip = np.abs(s - 1.0) > np.abs(s + 1.0)
        if flip.any():
            s[flip] = -s[flip]

        A_new = A * s
        if not mask.all():
            s0 = np.sqrt(C2[~mask])
            s0 = np.where(np.isfinite(s0), s0, 0.0j)
            A_new[~mask] = s0[~mask]

        A_new = clean_rfft(A_new, N)
        a_new = np.fft.irfft(A_new, N)[:n]
        a_new = np.where(np.isfinite(a_new), a_new, 0.0)
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
        S = float(a_new.sum())
        if S < 1e-12:
            break
        a = a_new / S

    final_sc = score_from_a(a, N)
    if final_sc < best_sc:
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_sc = final_sc
    return best_a, best_sc


def run_csurgery_passes(a, N, C_target, allocate, deadline, tag=""):
    a = make_nonneg_normalized(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_from_a(a, N)

    passes = [
        (0.05, 0.70, 0.30),
        (0.20, 0.85, 0.30),
        (0.40, 0.95, 0.25),
        (0.60, 1.00, 0.15),
    ]

    for ms, me, frac in passes:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 3.0:
            break
        sec = float(allocate) * float(frac)
        if sec < 2.0:
            continue
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + sec)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        a2, sc2 = run_csurgery(best_a, N, C_target, 100000,
                               ms, me, sub_deadline,
                               start_time=t0, duration=sec,
                               tag=tag + f" {ms:.2f}->{me:.2f}")
        if sc2 < best_sc:
            best_a = a2
            best_sc = sc2
    return best_a, best_sc


def run_peak_descent(a, N, Ngrad, max_iters, n_peaks, step_init, deadline, tag=""):
    a = make_nonneg_normalized(a)
    n = a.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    lb = 2.0 * n / float(2 * n - 1)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_from_a(a, N)
    step = max(1e-6, float(step_init))
    no_improve = 0

    for it in range(int(max_iters)):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break

        A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
        C = A * A
        c = np.fft.irfft(C, N)[:L]
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        sc = 2.0 * n * cmax

        if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_a = a.copy()
            best_sc = sc
            no_improve = 0
            if tag and it % 100 == 0:
                print(f"    {tag}: peak improve {sc:.8f} it={it}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve > 1200:
            break
        if sc <= lb + 1e-12:
            break

        k = int(n_peaks)
        if k >= L:
            p = np.full(L, 1.0 / L)
        else:
            top = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
            p = np.zeros(L)
            p[top] = 1.0 / float(k)

        p_rev = p[::-1]
        A2 = np.fft.rfft(a, Ngrad)
        P = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, Ngrad)
        r = np.fft.irfft(A2 * P, Ngrad)
        v = r[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
        grad = 4.0 * n * v
        g2 = float(np.dot(grad, grad))
        if g2 <= 1e-30:
            break

        max_step = (sc - lb) / (g2 + 1e-15)
        step_use = min(step, max(1e-7, max_step), 0.06)

        a2 = a - step_use * grad
        a2 = np.where(np.isfinite(a2), a2, 0.0)
        a2 = np.clip(a2, 0.0, 1000.0)
        S = float(a2.sum())
        if S < 1e-12:
            step *= 0.5
            continue
        a2 /= S

        sc2 = score_from_a(a2, N)
        if sc2 < sc - 1e-15:
            a = a2
            step = min(0.06, step * 1.01)
        else:
            step *= 0.72
            if step < 1e-7:
                break

    final_sc = score_from_a(a, N)
    if final_sc < best_sc:
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_sc = final_sc
    return best_a, best_sc


def run_peak_suite(a, N, Ngrad, allocate, deadline, tag=""):
    a = make_nonneg_normalized(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_from_a(a, N)

    configs = [
        (3, 0.020),
        (8, 0.015),
        (25, 0.010),
        (80, 0.005),
    ]
    m = len(configs)
    for i, (k, stp) in enumerate(configs):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        alloc = max(0.0, allocate) / float(m)
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        a2, sc2 = run_peak_descent(best_a, N, Ngrad, 100000, k, stp,
                                   sub_deadline, tag=f"{tag} peaks={k}")
        if sc2 < best_sc:
            best_a = a2
            best_sc = sc2
    return best_a, best_sc


def theta_to_softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size + 1
    theta = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    theta[0] = 0.0
    theta[1:] = x
    m = float(np.max(theta))
    e = np.exp(theta - m)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return e / s


def softmax_lse_fun(x, beta, N, Ngrad):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = x.size + 1
    zero_grad = np.zeros_like(x)
    if n < 2:
        return 1e100, zero_grad

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        return 1e100, zero_grad

    a = theta_to_softmax(x)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)) or a.sum() < 1e-15:
        return 1e100, zero_grad

    L = 2 * n - 1
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    C = A * A
    c = np.fft.irfft(C, N)[:L]
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if maxc <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(maxc):
        return 1e100, zero_grad

    beta = float(beta)
    shift = beta * (c - maxc)
    shift -= float(np.max(shift))
    expv = np.exp(shift)
    sumv = float(expv.sum())
    if sumv <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(sumv):
        expv = np.zeros(L)
        expv[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        sumv = 1.0

    Lsm = maxc + float(np.log(sumv)) / beta
    p = expv / sumv

    p_rev = p[::-1]
    A2 = np.fft.rfft(a, Ngrad)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, Ngrad)
    r = np.fft.irfft(A2 * P, Ngrad)
    v = r[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]

    grad_a = 4.0 * n * v
    dot = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    u = a * (grad_a - dot)

    val = 2.0 * n * Lsm
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(u)):
        return val, zero_grad
    grad = u[1:]
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return val, zero_grad
    return val, grad


def run_lbfgs(a, beta, N, Ngrad, time_limit, deadline, tag=""):
    a = make_nonneg_normalized(a)
    n = a.size
    sc0 = score_from_a(a, N)
    if n < 32:
        return a, sc0

    t_start = time.monotonic()
    remain = max(0.0, deadline - t_start)
    if remain < 8.0:
        return a, sc0

    eps = max(np.finfo(np.float64).tiny, 1e-12 * float(np.max(a)))
    theta = np.log(np.maximum(a, eps))
    x0 = np.clip(theta[1:] - theta[0], -50.0, 50.0)
    x0 = np.where(np.isfinite(x0), x0, 0.0)

    val0, g0 = softmax_lse_fun(x0, beta, N, Ngrad)
    if not np.isfinite(val0):
        return a, sc0

    t_call = max(1e-3, time.monotonic() - t_start)
    allocated = min(float(time_limit), max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()))
    if allocated < 8.0:
        return a, sc0

    if Ngrad > 49152:
        cap = 250
    elif N <= 8192:
        cap = 900
    else:
        cap = 600

    maxfun = int(allocated / (max(1e-3, t_call) * 1.7))
    maxfun = min(cap, max(50, maxfun))
    maxiter = min(maxfun, max(30, int(allocated / max(1e-3, t_call * 1.2))))

    res = minimize(
        softmax_lse_fun,
        x0,
        method="L-BFGS-B",
        jac=True,
        args=(beta, N, Ngrad),
        options={
            "maxiter": int(maxiter),
            "maxfun": int(maxfun),
            "ftol": 1e-13,
            "gtol": 1e-9,
            "maxls": 20,
            "disp": False,
        }
    )

    x1 = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel() if hasattr(res, "x") else x0
    x1 = np.where(np.isfinite(x1), x1, 0.0)
    a1 = make_nonneg_normalized(theta_to_softmax(x1))
    sc1 = score_from_a(a1, N)

    if np.isfinite(sc1) and sc1 < sc0:
        return a1, sc1
    return a, sc0


def run_lse_suite(a, N, Ngrad, allocate, deadline, tag=""):
    a = make_nonneg_normalized(a)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = score_from_a(a, N)

    n = a.size
    base = max(1000, 2 * n)
    betas = [min(int(base), 500000),
             min(int(4 * base), 500000),
             min(int(16 * base), 500000)]
    seen = []
    for b in betas:
        if b not in seen:
            seen.append(b)
    betas = seen

    m = len(betas)
    for i, b in enumerate(betas):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        alloc = max(0.0, allocate) / float(m)
        if alloc < 6.0:
            break
        sub_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + alloc)
        a2, sc2 = run_lbfgs(best_a, b, N, Ngrad, alloc, sub_deadline,
                            tag=f"{tag} beta={b}")
        if sc2 < best_sc:
            best_a = a2
            best_sc = sc2
    return best_a, best_sc


def process_length(n, parent, length_deadline, tag=""):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    if time.monotonic() > length_deadline - 30.0:
        return None, np.inf

    n = int(n)
    Nscore, Nopt, Ngrad = fft_lengths(n)
    C_main = make_target_rfft(n, Nopt, "rootbell")

    inits = build_inits(n, Nopt, C_main, parent)
    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    for idx, a0 in enumerate(inits):
        a0 = make_nonneg_normalized(a0)
        sc = score_from_a(a0, Nscore)
        print(f"  [{tag}] init {idx}: score={sc:.8f}")
        if sc < best_sc:
            best_a = a0
            best_sc = sc

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
        best_sc = score_from_a(best_a, Nscore)

    phase_total = max(60.0, length_deadline - t_start)

    use_lse = (n <= 10000 and Ngrad <= 65536)
    use_peak = (Ngrad <= 65536)

    alloc_quick = 0.08 * phase_total
    alloc_targets = 0.52 * phase_total
    alloc_peak = 0.16 * phase_total if use_peak else 0.0
    alloc_lse = 0.14 * phase_total if use_lse else 0.0

    # Quick diversified csurgery on initializers.
    quick_deadline = min(length_deadline, t_start + alloc_quick)
    per = max(1.5, alloc_quick / max(1, len(inits)))
    for a0 in inits:
        if time.monotonic() > quick_deadline - 1.0:
            break
        sub_deadline = min(quick_deadline, time.monotonic() + per)
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        a2, sc2 = run_csurgery(a0, Nopt, C_main, 100000, 0.10, 0.85,
                               sub_deadline, start_time=t0, duration=max(0.1, sub_deadline - t0),
                               tag=tag + ":quick")
        if sc2 < best_sc:
            best_a = a2
            best_sc = sc2
            print(f"  [{tag}] quick best: {best_sc:.8f}")

    # Main target-shape csurgery sweeps.
    reserve = 25.0 + alloc_peak + alloc_lse
    target_deadline = min(length_deadline, length_deadline - reserve)
    if target_deadline < time.monotonic() + 15.0:
        target_deadline = min(length_deadline, time.monotonic() + 1.0)

    target_modes = [
        ("rootbell", 1.00),
        ("cos05", 0.80),
        ("beta0.5", 0.70),
        ("flat", 0.50),
        ("cos1", 0.35),
    ]
    total_w = sum(w for _, w in target_modes)

    for mode, w in target_modes:
        if time.monotonic() > target_deadline - 8.0:
            break
        alloc = alloc_targets * (w / total_w)
        if alloc < 5.0:
            continue
        C = make_target_rfft(n, Nopt, mode)
        a2, sc2 = run_csurgery_passes(best_a, Nopt, C, alloc, target_deadline,
                                      tag=tag + f":{mode}")
        if sc2 < best_sc:
            best_a = a2
            best_sc = sc2
            print(f"  [{tag}] target {mode} best: {best_sc:.8f}")

    # Peak descent.
    if use_peak and time.monotonic() < length_deadline - 20.0 and alloc_peak > 0.0:
        left = max(0.0, length_deadline - time.monotonic())
        peak_alloc = min(alloc_peak + 0.25 * left, left)
        peak_deadline = min(length_deadline, time.monotonic() + peak_alloc)
        best_a, best_sc = run_peak_suite(best_a, Nscore, Ngrad, peak_alloc,
                                         peak_deadline, tag=tag + ":peak")
        if best_sc < np.inf:
            print(f"  [{tag}] after peak: {best_sc:.8f}")

    # LSE / L-BFGS polish.
    if use_lse and time.monotonic() < length_deadline - 15.0 and alloc_lse > 0.0:
        lse_deadline = min(length_deadline - 2.0, time.monotonic() + alloc_lse)
        best_a, best_sc = run_lse_suite(best_a, Nscore, Ngrad, alloc_lse,
                                        lse_deadline, tag=tag + ":lse")
        if best_sc < np.inf:
            print(f"  [{tag}] after LSE: {best_sc:.8f}")

    final_sc = score_from_a(best_a, Nscore)
    print(f"  [{tag}] FINAL for n={n}: {final_sc:.8f}")
    return best_a, final_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    final_deadline = t_start + total_budget - 25.0

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size >= 64:
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(p.sum()) > 1e-12:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    if parent is not None:
        pa = make_nonneg_normalized(parent)
        Np = next_pow2(2 * pa.size - 1)
        pa_sc = score_from_a(pa, Np)
        best_a = pa
        best_sc = pa_sc
        print(f"Parent initialized: n={pa.size}, score={pa_sc:.8f}")

    # Cheap explicit candidates as fallback / warm start.
    quick_ns = []
    if parent is not None and 1024 <= parent.size <= 20000:
        quick_ns.append(int(parent.size))
    if 8192 not in quick_ns:
        quick_ns.append(8192)

    for nn in quick_ns[:2]:
        Nq = next_pow2(2 * nn - 1)
        aq, sq = quick_beta_candidates(nn, Nq, (0.35, 0.45, 0.5, 0.6, 0.7))
        if sq < best_sc:
            best_a = aq
            best_sc = sq
            print(f"Quick Beta candidate n={nn}: score={sq:.8f}")

    jobs = []
    if parent is not None and 1024 <= parent.size <= 20000:
        jobs.append((int(parent.size), 0.30, "parent"))
    if 8192 not in [j[0] for j in jobs]:
        jobs.append((8192, 0.50 if parent is not None else 0.68, "main8192"))
    if total_budget > 1800.0:
        large = 12288
        if large not in [j[0] for j in jobs]:
            jobs.append((large, 0.20 if parent is not None else 0.24, "large12288"))

    total_w = sum(j[1] for j in jobs)
    remaining = max(0.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic())

    for idx, (nn, wt, tag) in enumerate(jobs):
        if time.monotonic() <= final_deadline - 60.0:
            frac = float(wt) / float(max(1e-12, total_w))
            if idx == len(jobs) - 1:
                length_deadline = final_deadline
            else:
                desired = frac * max(0.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic())
                length_deadline = min(final_deadline, time.monotonic() + max(60.0, desired))
            if time.monotonic() < length_deadline - 30.0:
                res_a, res_sc = process_length(nn, parent, length_deadline, tag=tag)
                if res_a is not None and res_sc < best_sc:
                    best_a = res_a
                    best_sc = res_sc
                    print(f"Job {tag} new global best: {best_sc:.8f} (n={len(best_a)})")
        remaining = max(0.0, final_deadline - time.monotonic())

    if best_a is None:
        if parent is not None:
            best_a = make_nonneg_normalized(parent)
        else:
            best_a = beta_like(4096, 0.5)
    else:
        best_a = make_nonneg_normalized(best_a)

    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, float(x)))) for x in best_a]
    while out and out[-1] == 0.0:
        out.pop()
    if not out or sum(out) < 0.01:
        out = [0.5] * 64

    verified = best_sc
    if len(out) <= 8192:
        try:
            v = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            if np.isfinite(v):
                verified = v
        except Exception:
            verified = best_sc

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL length={len(out)} internal={best_sc:.8f} verified={verified:.8f} time={elapsed:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 70)
    return out
```
